Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-3, -1, 2)
find y: int(0, 2, 4)
find a: bool
find b: bool
find c: bool

such that

sum([(product([3,x;int(1..)]) - product([2,y;int(1..)])),product([5,toInt(a);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) <= 1,
(sum([product([3,toInt(a);int(1..)]),product([5,toInt(b);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) - product([2,toInt(c);int(1..)])) >= 2,
sum([(product([2,toInt(a);int(1..)]) - product([3,toInt(b);int(1..)])),product([4,toInt(c);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) = sum([2,toInt(a);int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(a),toInt(a),toInt(!(a)),1,0;int(1..5)]) < 4

--

sum([(product([3,x;int(1..)]) - product([2,y;int(1..)])),product([5,toInt(a);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) <= 1,
(sum([product([3,toInt(a);int(1..)]),product([5,toInt(b);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) - product([2,toInt(c);int(1..)])) >= 2,
sum([(product([2,toInt(a);int(1..)]) - product([3,toInt(b);int(1..)])),product([4,toInt(c);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) = sum([2,toInt(a);int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(a),toInt(a),toInt(!(a)),1,0;int(1..5)]) < 4
   ~~> select_pseudo_boolean ([("SAT", 19000)])
sum([(product([3,x;int(1..)]) - product([2,y;int(1..)])),product([5,toInt(a);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) <= 1,
sum([(product([2,toInt(a);int(1..)]) - product([3,toInt(b);int(1..)])),product([4,toInt(c);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) = sum([2,toInt(a);int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(a),toInt(a),toInt(!(a)),1,0;int(1..5)]) < 4
new SAT encoding decisions:
  weighted(3*a, 5*b, -2*c) AtLeast 2 using None

new SAT encoding decisions:
